402: async function withStartupTimeout(promise, milliseconds, fallback = null) {
403:   let timer;
404:   try {
405:     return await Promise.race([
406:       Promise.resolve(promise),
407:       new Promise((resolve) => { timer = setTimeout(() => resolve(fallback), milliseconds); }),
408:     ]);
409:   } finally {
410:     if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
411:   }
412: }

632: async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
633:   if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
634:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
635:   url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
636:   url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
637:   url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
638:   ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
639:   const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
640:   if (!response.ok) {
641:     let detail = "";
642:     try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
643:     if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
644:       const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
645:       const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
646:       await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
647:       return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);

248: function recoveryKey(salt) {
249:   // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
250:   // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
251:   const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
252:   return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
253: }
254: 
255: async function writeCredentialRecovery(value) {
256:   const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
257:   const key = recoveryKey(salt);
258:   const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
259:   const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
260:   const payload = {
261:     version: 1, algorithm: "aes-256-gcm",
262:     salt: salt.toString("base64"), iv: iv.toString("base64"),
263:     tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"), data: encrypted.toString("base64")
264:   };
265:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(credentialRecoveryPath()), { recursive: true });
266:   await fs.writeFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8");
